# W0 current-source baseline and lane audit

This is the current-source W0 checkpoint for the Astra goal. It deliberately does
not promote any implementation, performance result, or product capability to
`accepted`. The checkout contains foreign and uncommitted work from many owners;
all evidence below is therefore tied to the commands and fingerprints recorded
here, not to `HEAD` alone.

stash, or commit another owner’s work. The current WOC adapter depends on symbols
absent from the pinned VM `HEAD`, so the live dirty tree cannot be represented as
a passing managed receipt.

## Clean VM archive diagnostic (2026-09-14)

An older isolated validation root at
`E:\Git\astra-validation-20260905` contains a clean sibling `zr_vm` checkout
at `4ab8ddbc9d180eb5042e5b83b24deee66c91d7ca` and the required sibling mount
name. It is useful provenance, but it is not a current validation input: its
ZirconEngine snapshot is `743f3680aa88d4665aa74f5b42250d76d507e270`, not the
current shared checkout, and its historical coordinator object store no longer
contains the sealed external archive payload.

A disposable coordinator probe submitted the clean VM descriptor for a
validation-copy Cargo metadata request. The request was rejected at
`artifact_governance` before source materialization with
`unmanaged_artifacts_detected` for the shared target roots
`E:\cargo-targets\zircon-engine\pool` and
`F:\cargo-targets\zircon-engine\ephemeral`. No target directory, dependency,
or coordinator policy was changed to bypass that fence; the temporary daemon
was stopped and its port was verified closed. This is a diagnostic receipt only,
not a native validation or a substitute for an owned, current, sealed VM input.

sample observed ZirconEngine `HEAD` `982f8a70b33c6cdbb8b2b62a4e73dca07c530223`,
`origin/main` `6b4bc86089cb4464f850136c8079e90cb6513ebc`, and 8,221
porcelain paths. The external `E:/Git/zr_vm` sample observed `HEAD`
`0ee3b3b39df989f2cb3df0b2cc764fd39aff9694` and 192 dirty paths. Its current
binding `lib.rs` exports `execution_budget` and `project_session_checkpoint`,
so the older statement that the pinned VM `HEAD` lacks those symbols must not be
applied to this newer `HEAD`. The 22 dirty Rust binding files still need an
immutable, dependency-complete validation input. This sampling supplies no
managed Cargo, native VM, product or performance acceptance.

### Core/Runtime lifecycle, App/headless and WOC/VM source crosswalk (2026-09-26)

The three [APP-LAUNCH-P0-001/002/003](../../../optimize/zircon_app/09-product-launch-request-cli-config-resolution-provenance-diagnostics-exit-semantics-current-working-tree-review.md)
rows above are refreshed in place; they remain open and are not counted again in
the nine rows below. [Runtime157](../../../optimize/zircon_runtime/157-runtime-core-module-lifecycle-registry-service-resolution-shutdown-current-source-review.md)
explicitly refreshes Runtime01, while Runtime72 and Runtime301 are separate
report-qualified reviews with overlapping product shutdown concerns; no direct
Runtime157-to-Runtime72 currentness edge was found. App03's WOC findings have no
confirmed formal refresh edge; later App05 and Astra product-host records narrow
their current source status without accepting the original product gate.

The dependency order is source-set closure and generator consistency, then an
immutable external VM and Rust binding input, then managed native/role tests,
and finally real product and release measurements. Runtime owner/task/session
shutdown receipts precede App headless terminal acceptance. The read-only WOC
source check found [root `examples/*` ignore](../../../../../.gitignore) still in
effect, ten referenced Rust protocol modules ignored/untracked, and 33 reachable
`.zr` modules from the tracked script entry ignored/untracked. These current
worktree counts update App03's older 29-module snapshot; they are a source-closure
signal, not a clean-clone build or canonical deduplication receipt.

## M0.1 tooling gate attempt

The narrow, dependency-independent gate was attempted before any engine or VM
implementation work:

```text
pwsh -NoProfile -File tools/tests/session-coordinator-smoke.Tests.ps1
```

Result: **failed before assertions** with `Coordinator did not become healthy.`
The temporary coordinator process remained alive and wrote its SQLite state, but
the smoke loop did not observe health within its fixed 50 x 100 ms window. A
separate diagnostic launch using the same coordinator module with `--port 0`
did write a valid `runtime.json` descriptor after the startup window, proving the
failure is a launch/readiness timing/environment issue rather than evidence that
the JSON contract passed. No production source was changed to mask the failure,
and no M0.1 milestone was closed.

The historical M0.1 record under `docs/plans/mvp/00/` is retained as historical
evidence only. Current MVP `00` remains `in_progress`; F0-F5 remain serially
blocked. The next retry should claim the existing M0.1 tooling scope, capture
cold and warm daemon readiness with logs, then run the full declared Pester and
Python focused suites and assert zero residual jobs/leases. It must refresh the
existing M0.1 child record rather than create a duplicate narrative.

Dependency-independent follow-up probes did produce bounded receipts, but they
do not repair the failed readiness gate:

- `-CargoAndCleanup` passed (`115` Python unit tests, `OK`, plus scheduled-task
  dry-run checks); `-JsonClient`, `-StrictJsonParser`, and `-ValidatorDryRun`
  passed. `-KernelOnly`, `-LeaseAndPatch`, and `-LegacyRollout` did not produce
  terminal green receipts because the temporary coordinator readiness loop
  expired (the long-running Legacy process was stopped after diagnosis).
- The Hub web batch
  `node --test web/tests/catalog_service.test.mjs
  web/tests/catalog_service_browser.test.mjs web/tests/account_state.test.mjs
  web/tests/hub_ui_regressions.test.mjs` exited `0`: `91` tests, `63` pass,
  `28` expected skips, `0` failures. A pre-existing WebSocket port warning was
  non-fatal. This is browser/source evidence only, not Rust/Tauri acceptance.
- Static/TDD receipts for explicit plugin admission, the handshake-bound WSS
  verifier, ED-A5 width bridging, and base/projected hit-grid cold atomicity
  are recorded in their child/session records and remain
  `implemented_pending_validation`.
- The ASSET-A4 manifest-save child record and AI behavior-tree budget child
  record are also `implemented_pending_validation`; both passed scoped static
  checks and deliberately did not run Cargo/native commands.

The independent review also identified a new RG-A3 P1 candidate: residency
drains resource events using the pre-artifact dependency index, so an event for
a dependency introduced by the same frame's scene artifact may be consumed
before the projector journal adds that dependency. A 2026-09-13 source recheck
now confirms the regression
`render_scene_resource_geometry_replay_keeps_same_frame_declared_and_supplemental_dependency_events`
in `zircon_runtime/src/graphics/scene/resources/resource_streamer/geometry_replay.rs`.
It exercises both the declared model and supplemental child-mesh event, then
retains both revisions after projection. This is source/test evidence only:
managed Cargo, resolver/journal execution, and Windows Release performance
evidence remain pending, so no acceptance is claimed for the revision-aware
replay slice.

An additional managed Windows attempt on 2026-09-13 used the focused
`zircon_runtime` `ui` test command with `--locked` and the coordinator-selected
`E:\cargo-targets` pool. The coordinator rejected the request before Cargo with
`unmanaged_artifacts_detected` because existing `F:\cargo-targets` artifact
directories (`ephemeral\debug-zui-state` and the prior validation scratch)
were not registered for this request. No artifact was deleted or altered; the
RG-A3 gate therefore remains pending rather than being treated as a test
failure or acceptance.

### 2026-09-27 MVP-00 M0.2 source-only validation risk

[Runtime04 migration commandlet link/reparse tests](../../../../../zircon_runtime/src/asset/tests/migration/project_commandlet/source_boundary.rs#L210) (SHA-256 `bb2033691865cca123e32a0fdc21a53245c03c2b16b6bc7db331f985d720d04c`) have eight fixture-admission early returns at lines 216, 256, 303, 345, 384, 421, 448 and 477. The Windows helper returns `false` for `PermissionDenied` or OS error 1314 (lines 506–536); the Unix helper also reduces link creation to `is_ok()`. A green run can therefore omit those eight link/reparse assertions without reporting skipped coverage. This is a **test proof hole**, not evidence of a production migration defect or an observed failed test. The stable Runtime04 repair Session registration request `3493b6b27e134bbba6d0f0b38c302ff6` was terminally rejected at 18:21:42 UTC with `plan_wip_limit_reached` because `failure-roll-01a084c8-runtime04-vg-project-toml-r3` remains the stale primary; preserve that owner's gates and scope. The supported next action is legal owner succession or an approved child role, then a strict test-local fixture failure and managed M0.2 run that **observes execution** of all eight cases. The foreign MVP-00 primary `astra-optimize-20260926-batch-a` is unchanged. M0.2 and M0.3 remain open; no source or failure artifact was changed, and no new output record was created.

### 2026-09-28 Hub03 local-service P0 source crosswalk

The [Hub03 original](../../../optimize/zircon_hub/03-marketplace-account-auth-organization-cloud-repository-provider-review.md) is unchanged at SHA-256 `55f6297d3217337d0f367cc488d9afb9041375e1ce07ff1bf6a9f8eb17f305f9`. That observation does not prove a current Hub compile. The following **selected ten source anchors** use ordered `path|sha256` lines joined by LF (without a final LF), with SHA-256 `d52e0e3780745ba52f46bfe60cc30bfc76f8594b2327a71f1a3488146c928344`. ## Unread and excluded scope

This W0 checkpoint is an evidence/ownership baseline, not a claim that every
historical optimize finding has been read or deduplicated. The following remain
explicitly unread or excluded from this checkpoint:

- full historical `docs/plans/optimize` finding-by-finding deduplication;
- managed Runtime, Editor, App, WGPU, Release, product-window, GPU, RSS/VRAM,
  and p50/p95/p99 measurements;
- clean validation-copy acceptance for the dirty `zr_vm` dependency and its
  nested gitlinks;
- unowned or waiting-validation source paths held by other sessions;
- advanced W5/W6 provider implementation beyond the read-only triage above;
- final W7/W8 Hub/Tauri/package-install/cloud-conflict and long-run evidence.

These exclusions are intentional. They keep the current source snapshot
auditable without overwriting foreign changes or treating stale `.codex/state`
logs as acceptance receipts.

## Exit status

W0 is **not accepted**. The durable next actions are:

1. reconcile the completed M0.1 smoke receipt with the readiness-race history
   and retain its long maintenance-scan duration as a diagnostic baseline;
2. obtain an owned, clean, submodule-complete VM receipt before native Cargo;
3. reconcile the M0.1 residual job/lease receipts and keep the full smoke
   matrix green; no M0.1 probe is now outstanding locally;
4. repair and revalidate the RG-A3 same-frame dependency-event candidate,
   preserve the completed plugin alias and Hub quota slices as
   `implemented_pending_validation`, and then batch their managed package tests;
5. obtain owners for the remaining asset compound, lifecycle ABI, ED-A6, Hub
   cloud/native, UI radius, provider, and WOC gaps without editing foreign
   scopes; only after the external VM admission is clean run the managed
   Windows Runtime/Editor/App and product/performance gates in dependency order.
